5852: const RenderCameraRayGenerator=Object.freeze({
5853:   rotateByQuaternion(v,q){
5854:     const x=v[0],y=v[1],z=v[2],qx=q[0],qy=q[1],qz=q[2],qw=q[3];
5855:     const ix=qw*x+qy*z-qz*y,iy=qw*y+qz*x-qx*z,iz=qw*z+qx*y-qy*x,iw=-qx*x-qy*y-qz*z;
5856:     return [
5857:       ix*qw+iw*-qx+iy*-qz-iz*-qy,
5858:       iy*qw+iw*-qy+iz*-qx-ix*-qz,
5859:       iz*qw+iw*-qz+ix*-qy-iy*-qx
5860:     ];
5861:   },
5862:   ray(cameraSnapshot,px,py,width,height){
5863:     const c=RenderCameraPolicy.sanitize(cameraSnapshot);
5864:     width=Math.max(1,Number(width)||1);height=Math.max(1,Number(height)||1);
5865:     const nx=((px+.5)/width)*2-1;
5866:     const ny=1-((py+.5)/height)*2;
5867:     const aspect=Math.max(1e-6,width/height);
5868:     const fovRad=c.fov*Math.PI/180,tan=Math.tan(fovRad*.5);
5869:     const local=RayMath.normalize([nx*aspect*tan,ny*tan,-1]);
5870:     const world=RayMath.normalize(this.rotateByQuaternion(local,c.quaternion));
5871:     return new RenderRay(c.position,world,c.near,c.far,RenderRayType.CAMERA,0);
5872:   }
5873: });

5875: var RenderAccelerationSet = class RenderAccelerationSet{
5876:   constructor(store,bvh){
5877:     this.store=store;this.bvh=bvh;
5878:     this.vertexCount=store.positions.length/3;
5879:     this.triangleCount=store.triangleCount();
5880:     this.nodeCount=bvh.nodes.length;
5881:     this.stats=Object.freeze({geometry:store.stats,bvh:bvh.stats()});
5882:     Object.freeze(this);
5883:   }
5884:   trace(ray){return this.bvh.intersect(ray)}
5885:   occluded(ray){return this.bvh.anyHit(ray)}
5886: }

5889: const RenderAccelerationCache16={
5890:   geometryRevision:null,
5891:   acceleration:null,
5892:   hits:0,
5893:   misses:0,
5894:   reset(){this.geometryRevision=null;this.acceleration=null;this.hits=0;this.misses=0;},
5895:   invalidate(){this.geometryRevision=null;this.acceleration=null;},
5896:   revision(scene){
5897:     const m=scene?.metadata||{};
5898:     return [m.sceneRevision??'s?',m.geometryRevision??'g?',m.materialRevision??'m?',m.lightRevision??'l?',scene?.objects?.length||0,
5899:       ...(scene?.objects||[]).map(o=>`${o.id}:${o.visible!==false?1:0}:${o.mesh?.positions?.length||0}:${o.mesh?.indices?.length||0}`)
5900:     ].join('|');
5901:   },
5902:   get(scene){
5903:     const rev=this.revision(scene);
5904:     if(this.acceleration&&this.geometryRevision===rev){this.hits++;return this.acceleration;}
5905:     this.misses++;return null;
5906:   },
5907:   put(scene,acceleration){
5908:     this.geometryRevision=this.revision(scene);
5909:     this.acceleration=acceleration;
5910:     return acceleration;
5911:   },
5912:   stats(){return Object.freeze({geometryRevision:this.geometryRevision,hits:this.hits,misses:this.misses,reused:!!this.acceleration});}
5913: };

5915: /*
5916:   BLAS/TLAS boundary:
5917:   - object mesh geometry revisions feed BLAS-equivalent cached acceleration data;
5918:   - scene/object transform revisions are intentionally kept outside this cache key
5919:     so a future TLAS can update instances without rebuilding object geometry.
5920:   Current browser renderer still uses the proven binary RenderAccelerationSet.
5921: */
5922: const RenderAccelerationHierarchy16=Object.freeze({
5923:   mode:'geometry-revision acceleration cache; TLAS/BLAS boundary reserved',
5924:   cache:RenderAccelerationCache16,
5925:   build(scene,builder){
5926:     const cached=RenderAccelerationCache16.get(scene);
5927:     if(cached)return cached;
5928:     return RenderAccelerationCache16.put(scene,builder());
5929:   }
5930: });
5931: window.RenderAccelerationCache16=RenderAccelerationCache16;
5932: window.RenderAccelerationHierarchy16=RenderAccelerationHierarchy16;

7409: const AreaLightSampler=Object.freeze({
7410:   sample(light,point,u=.5,v=.5){
7411:     if(light.type==='rectangle'){
7412:       const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
7413:       return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
7414:     }
7415:     if(light.type==='disc'){
7416:       const lp=LightBasis.pointOnDisc(light,u,v);
7417:       return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
7418:     }
7419:     if(light.type==='sphere'){
7420:       const lp=LightBasis.pointOnSphere(light,u,v);
7421:       const normal=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(lp,light.position));
7422:       return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),normal);
7423:     }
7424:     if(light.type==='mesh'){
7425:       // Part 4 keeps Mesh Light as a rectangular proxy.
7426:       // True emissive mesh-triangle sampling remains Group 10.
7427:       const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
7428:       return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
7429:     }
7430:     return DirectLightSampler.sample(light,point);
7431:   },
7432: 
7433:   _local(light,point,lightPoint,area,emitterNormal=null){
7434:     const to=RayMath.sub(lightPoint,point);
7435:     const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-6);
7436:     const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);
7437: 
7438:     if(light.range>0&&dist>=light.range){
7439:       return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area};
7440:     }
7441: 
7442:     let attenuation=LightEnergyPolicy.distanceAttenuation(light,dist);
7443: 
7444:     let emitterCos=1;
7445:     if(emitterNormal){
7446:       emitterCos=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(RayMath.normalize(emitterNormal),RayMath.mul(dir,-1)));
7447:       if(emitterCos<=0){
7448:         return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area,emitterCos:0};
7449:       }
7450:     }
7451: 
7452:     const safeArea=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,area);
7453:     return {
7454:       kind:'local',
7455:       direction:dir,
7456:       distance:dist,
7457:       // Size changes sampling footprint/softness only; intensity remains the same user-facing unit.
7458:       radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation*emitterCos),
7459:       lightPoint,
7460:       area:safeArea,
7461:       emitterCos
7462:     };
7463:   }
7464: });

7654: function validateRenderGroup7(){
7655:   const errors=[];
7656:   if(typeof ShadowVisibility.visible!=='function')errors.push('ShadowVisibility missing');
7657:   if(typeof AreaLightSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('AreaLightSampler missing');
7658:   if(typeof DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('DirectLightingIntegrator missing');
7659:   if(typeof EnvironmentSampler.background!=='function')errors.push('EnvironmentSampler missing');
7660:   const tests=runRenderGroup7SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
7661:   return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
7662: }

6443: var RenderControlToken16 = class RenderControlToken16{
6444:   constructor(){
6445:     this.shared=typeof SharedArrayBuffer!=='undefined'&&typeof Atomics!=='undefined';
6446:     this.buffer=this.shared?new SharedArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3):new ArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3);
6447:     this.flags=new Int32Array(this.buffer); // [cancel,pause,generation]
6448:     this.flags[2]=1;
6449:   }
6450:   cancel(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,0,1):(this.flags[0]=1);}
6451:   reset(){this.shared?(Atomics.store(this.flags,0,0),Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0),Atomics.add(this.flags,2,1)):(this.flags[0]=0,this.flags[1]=0,this.flags[2]++);}
6452:   pause(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,1):(this.flags[1]=1);}
6453:   resume(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0):(this.flags[1]=0);}
6454:   cancelled(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,0):this.flags[0])!==0;}
6455:   paused(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,1):this.flags[1])!==0;}
6456:   generation(){return this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,2):this.flags[2];}
6457: }

6468:     this.stageMs={snapshot:0,geometry:0,worker:0,bvh:0,shading:0,lightCache:0,render:0,denoise:0,toneMap:0,post:0};
6469:     this.startedAt=RenderProfiler16.now();
6470:   }
6471:   addRay(type){
6472:     if(type===RenderRayType.CAMERA)this.cameraRays++;
6473:     else if(type===RenderRayType.SHADOW)this.shadowRays++;
6474:     else this.pathRays++;
6475:   }
6476:   addProbe(){this.probeRays++;}
6477:   markStage(name,start){
6478:     return RenderProfiler16.mark(this,name,start);
6479:   }
6480:   snapshot(){
6481:     const elapsed=Math.max(.001,performance.now()-this.startedAt);
6482:     const totalRays=this.cameraRays+this.pathRays;
6483:     return Object.freeze({
6484:       cameraRays:this.cameraRays,
6485:       pathRays:this.pathRays,
6486:       shadowRays:this.shadowRays,
6487:       probeRays:this.probeRays,
6488:       totalRays:totalRays+this.shadowRays+this.probeRays,
6489:       samples:this.samples,
6490:       yields:this.yields,
6491:       raysPerSecond:Math.round(totalRays/(elapsed/1000)),
6492:       stageMs:Object.freeze({...this.stageMs})
6493:     });
6494:   }

8092:     if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){

8096:       return (distance*distance)/(area*cosEmitter);
8097:     }
8098:     return 0;

8101:   evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1){
8102:     const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
8103:     const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
8104:     const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
8105:     let color=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);
8106:     const maxShadowRays=Math.max(1,Math.min(4096,Number(job?.settings?.maxShadowRaysPerHit)||1024));

8108:

8124:       let accum=[0,0,0];

8129:           ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
8130:           : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

8183: class RenderLightCache{
8184:   constructor(job,acceleration){
8185:     this.radius=LightCachePolicy.radius(job,acceleration);
8186:     this.cellSize=this.radius;
8187:     this.maxRecords=LightCachePolicy.maxRecords(job);
8188:     this.records=[];
8189:     this.grid=new Map();
8190:     this.hits=0;this.misses=0;this.inserts=0;this.rejected=0;this.retraces=0;this.refreshes=0;
8191:   }
8192:   key(ix,iy,iz){return ix+','+iy+','+iz}
8193:   cell(p){return [Math.floor(p[0]/this.cellSize),Math.floor(p[1]/this.cellSize),Math.floor(p[2]/this.cellSize)]}
8194:   insert(position,normal,radiance){
8195:     if(this.records.length>=this.maxRecords){this.rejected++;return false;}
8196:     if(!position?.every(Number.isFinite)||!normal?.every(Number.isFinite)||!radiance?.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0)){this.rejected++;return false;}
8197:     const rec=Object.freeze({position:position.slice(),normal:RayMath.normalize(normal),radiance:radiance.slice()});
8198:     const id=this.records.length;this.records.push(rec);this.inserts++;
8199:     const c=this.cell(position),k=this.key(c[0],c[1],c[2]);
8200:     let bucket=this.grid.get(k);if(!bucket)this.grid.set(k,bucket=[]);
8201:     bucket.push(id);return true;
8202:   }
8203:   query(position,normal){
8204:     const c=this.cell(position);let sum=[0,0,0],ws=0;
8205:     for(let z=-1;z<=1;z++)for(let y=-1;y<=1;y++)for(let x=-1;x<=1;x++){
8206:       const bucket=this.grid.get(this.key(c[0]+x,c[1]+y,c[2]+z));if(!bucket)continue;
8207:       for(const id of bucket){
8208:         const r=this.records[id];
8209:         const delta=RayMath.sub(r.position,position),dist=RayMath.length(delta);
8210:         if(dist>this.radius)continue;
8211:         const nd=RayMath.dot(r.normal,normal);if(nd<LightCachePolicy.normalThreshold)continue;
8212:         const w=(1-dist/this.radius)*Math.max(.05,nd);
8213:         sum=RenderColor.add(sum,RenderColor.mul(r.radiance,w));ws+=w;

7433:   _local(light,point,lightPoint,area,emitterNormal=null){
7434:     const to=RayMath.sub(lightPoint,point);
7435:     const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-6);
7436:     const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);
7437: 
7438:     if(light.range>0&&dist>=light.range){
7439:       return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area};
7440:     }

7442:     let attenuation=LightEnergyPolicy.distanceAttenuation(light,dist);
7443: 
7444:     let emitterCos=1;
7445:     if(emitterNormal){
7446:       emitterCos=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(RayMath.normalize(emitterNormal),RayMath.mul(dir,-1)));
7447:       if(emitterCos<=0){
7448:         return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area,emitterCos:0};
7449:       }
7450:     }
7451: 
7452:     const safeArea=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,area);
7453:     return {
7454:       kind:'local',
7455:       direction:dir,
7456:       distance:dist,
7457:       // Size changes sampling footprint/softness only; intensity remains the same user-facing unit.
7458:       radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation*emitterCos),
7459:       lightPoint,

17658: function frameImportedObjects(meshes){
17659:   const valid=(meshes||[]).filter(m=>m?.isMesh && m.geometry);
17660:   if(!valid.length)return;
17661: 
17662:   const box=new THREE.Box3();
17663:   let any=false;
17664:   for(const mesh of valid){
17665:     mesh.updateMatrixWorld(true);
17666:     const b=new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(mesh);
17667:     if(!b.isEmpty()){
17668:       if(!any){box.copy(b);any=true;}
17669:       else box.union(b);
17670:     }
17671:   }
17672:   if(!any || box.isEmpty())return;
17673: 
17674:   const center=box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
17675:   const size=box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3());
17676:   const radius=Math.max(size.length()*.5,1);
17677:   const dir=new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(camera.position,orbit.target);
17678:   if(dir.lengthSq()<1e-8)dir.set(1,1,.75);
17679:   dir.normalize();
17680: 
17681:   const fov=THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(camera.fov||45);
17682:   const fitDist=Math.max(radius/Math.sin(Math.max(fov*.5,0.1)), radius*2.2);
17683:   orbit.target.copy(center);
17684:   camera.position.copy(center).addScaledVector(dir,fitDist*1.15);
17685:   camera.near=Math.max(0.001,fitDist/10000);
17686:   camera.far=Math.max(10000,fitDist*100);
17687:   camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
17688:   orbit.update();
17689: }

17730: function combineImportedMeshes(meshes,fileName,opts){
17731:   // Compact native combine path using BufferGeometry merge by transforming
17732:   // positions into world space and concatenating non-indexed triangle streams.
17733:   const positions=[],normals=[],uvs=[],colors=[];
17734:   let hasUV=false,hasColor=false;
17735:   for(const src of meshes){
17736:     src.updateMatrixWorld(true);
17737:     let g=src.geometry.clone();
17738:     if(g.index)g=g.toNonIndexed();
17739:     const p=g.getAttribute('position'),n=g.getAttribute('normal'),uv=g.getAttribute('uv'),col=g.getAttribute('color');
17740:     const normalMatrix=new THREE.Matrix3().getNormalMatrix(src.matrixWorld);
17741:     for(let i=0;i<p.count;i++){
17742:       const v=new THREE.Vector3().fromBufferAttribute(p,i).applyMatrix4(src.matrixWorld);
17743:       positions.push(v.x,v.y,v.z);
17744:       if(n){
17745:         const vn=new THREE.Vector3().fromBufferAttribute(n,i).applyMatrix3(normalMatrix).normalize();
17746:         normals.push(vn.x,vn.y,vn.z);
17747:       }
17748:       if(uv){hasUV=true;uvs.push(uv.getX(i),uv.getY(i));}else if(hasUV)uvs.push(0,0);
17749:       if(col){hasColor=true;colors.push(col.getX(i),col.getY(i),col.getZ(i));}else if(hasColor)colors.push(1,1,1);
17750:     }
17751:   }
17752:   const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry();
17753:   g.setAttribute('position',new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions,3));
17754:   if(normals.length===positions.length)g.setAttribute('normal',new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(normals,3));
17755:   if(hasUV&&uvs.length===positions.length/3*2)g.setAttribute('uv',new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uvs,2));
17756:   if(hasColor&&colors.length===positions.length)g.setAttribute('color',new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(colors,3));
17757:   if(!g.attributes.normal)g.computeVertexNormals();
17758:   const mesh=new THREE.Mesh(g,makeObjectMaterial(COLORS.object));
17759:   mesh.name=importedMeshName(fileName);
17760:   return prepareImportedMesh(mesh,fileName,opts);
17761: }
17762: function finalizeImportedMesh(mesh,opts){
17763:   // Match 3DLite editor units: centimeters.
17764:   const factor=importUnitFactor(opts.units)*opts.scale;
17765:   mesh.scale.multiplyScalar(factor);
17766: 
17767:   // 3DLite uses Z-Up. Convert explicitly marked Y-Up content.
17768:   if(opts.upAxis==='y'){
17769:     mesh.rotation.x+=Math.PI/2;
17770:   }
17771: 
17772:   // Normalize geometry around its own center so the transform gizmo/pivot is centered.
17773:   if(opts.center){
17774:     mesh.updateMatrixWorld(true);
17775:     const localBox=new THREE.Box3().setFromBufferAttribute(mesh.geometry.getAttribute('position'));
17776:     const localCenter=localBox.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
17777:     mesh.geometry.translate(-localCenter.x,-localCenter.y,-localCenter.z);
17778:   }
17779: 
17780:   // Default imported transform in editor space: position 0,0,0 with identity rotation/scale.
17781:   if(opts.resetTransform){
17782:     mesh.updateMatrix();
17783:     mesh.geometry.applyMatrix4(mesh.matrix);

===== isVisible @ 17831 =====
17796:     }
17797:   }
17798: 
17799:   rebuildWire(mesh);
17800: 
17801:   if(opts.importAs==='editable'){
17802:     geometryToEditablePoly(mesh);
17803:   }
17804:   return mesh;
17805: }
17806: function addImportedRoot(root,fileName,opts){
17807:   root.updateMatrixWorld(true);
17808:   let source=importedMeshesFromRoot(root);
17809:   if(!source.length){hintEl.textContent='Import: no mesh objects found in '+fileName;return;}
17810: 
17811:   const imported=[];
17812:   if(opts.structure==='combine'){
17813:     const combined=combineImportedMeshes(source,fileName,opts);
17814:     if(combined)imported.push(combined);
17815:   }else{
17816:     // "Combine by Material" currently groups conservatively as separate meshes
17817:     // when material identity cannot be safely merged without losing sub-material data.
17818:     for(const child of source){
17819:       const wp=new THREE.Vector3(),wq=new THREE.Quaternion(),ws=new THREE.Vector3();
17820:       child.matrixWorld.decompose(wp,wq,ws);
17821:       const mesh=prepareImportedMesh(child,fileName,opts);
17822:       if(!mesh)continue;
17823:       mesh.position.copy(wp);mesh.quaternion.copy(wq);mesh.scale.copy(ws);
17824:       imported.push(mesh);
17825:     }
17826:   }
17827: 
17828:   const visibleImported=[];
17829:   for(const mesh of imported){
17830:     finalizeImportedMesh(mesh,opts);
17831:     if(!importedGeometryIsVisible(mesh.geometry)){
17832:       console.warn('3DLite import produced empty/invalid geometry after finalize:',mesh.name||fileName);
17833:       continue;
17834:     }
17835:     if(mesh.parent)mesh.parent.remove(mesh);
17836:     mesh.visible=true;
17837:     mesh.frustumCulled=true;
17838:     scene.add(mesh);objects.push(mesh);
17839:     visibleImported.push(mesh);
17840:     pushUndo({type:'create',object:mesh});
17841:   }
17842: 
17843:   if(visibleImported.length){
17844:     selectOnly(visibleImported[visibleImported.length-1]);
17845:     // Keep the transform gizmo centered on the selected imported object's pivot.
17846:     syncTransformTarget?.();
17847:     frameImportedObjects(visibleImported);
17848:     hintEl.textContent='Imported '+visibleImported.length+' object'+(visibleImported.length===1?'':'s')+' and framed in viewport';
17849:     updateStats();
17850:   }else{
17851:     hintEl.textContent='Import failed: OBJ contained no visible mesh after cleanup';
17852:   }
17853: }
17854: function importObjectFile(file,opts=getImportOptions()){
17855:   if(!file)return;
17856:   const ext=(file.name.split('.').pop()||'').toLowerCase();
17857:   const reader=new FileReader();
17858:   reader.onerror=()=>hintEl.textContent='Import failed: could not read '+file.name;
17859: 
17860:   if(ext==='obj'){
17861:     reader.onload=()=>{
17862:       try{
17863:         const root=new THREE.OBJLoader().parse(String(reader.result||''));
17864:         addImportedRoot(root,file.name,opts);
17865:       }catch(err){console.error(err);hintEl.textContent='OBJ import failed: '+err.message;}
17866:     };
17867:     reader.readAsText(file);
17868:   }else if(ext==='fbx'){
17869:     reader.onload=()=>{
17870:       try{
17871:         const root=new THREE.FBXLoader().parse(reader.result,'');
17872:         addImportedRoot(root,file.name,opts);
17873:       }catch(err){console.error(err);hintEl.textContent='FBX import failed: '+err.message;}
17874:     };
17875:     reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
17876:   }else{
17877:     hintEl.textContent='Import supports .OBJ and .FBX files.';
17878:   }
17879: }

8419: class ProgressiveAccumulator{
8420:   constructor(width,height){
8421:     this.width=width;this.height=height;
8422:     const n=width*height;
8423:     this.sum=new Float64Array(n*3);
8424:     this.mean=new Float64Array(n);
8425:     this.m2=new Float64Array(n);
8426:     this.samples=new Uint32Array(n);
8427:     this.converged=new Uint8Array(n);
8428:     this.active=n;
8429:   }
8430:   add(index,color){
8431:     const s=++this.samples[index];
8432:     const o=index*3;
8433:     this.sum[o]+=color[0];this.sum[o+1]+=color[1];this.sum[o+2]+=color[2];
8434:     const lum=.2126*color[0]+.7152*color[1]+.0722*color[2];
8435:     const delta=lum-this.mean[index];
8436:     this.mean[index]+=delta/s;
8437:     this.m2[index]+=delta*(lum-this.mean[index]);
8438:   }
8439:   variance(index){
8440:     const s=this.samples[index];
8441:     return s>1?Math.max(0,this.m2[index]/(s-1)):Infinity;
8442:   }
8443:   noise(index){
8444:     const mean=Math.max(1e-6,Math.abs(this.mean[index]));
8445:     const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[index]);
8446:     return Math.sqrt(this.variance(index)/s)/mean;
8447:   }
8448:   shouldSample(index,settings){
8449:     if(!settings.adaptive)return this.samples[index]<settings.maxSamples;
8450:     if(this.samples[index]<settings.minSamples)return true;
8451:     if(this.samples[index]>=settings.maxSamples)return false;
8452:     return this.noise(index)>settings.noiseThreshold;
8453:   }
8454:   markConverged(index){
8455:     if(this.converged[index])return;
8456:     this.converged[index]=1;this.active=Math.max(0,this.active-1);
8457:   }
8458:   resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask){
8459:     const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(this.width*this.height*4);
8460:     for(let i=0;i<this.width*this.height;i++){
8461:       const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[i]),o=i*3,p=i*4;
8462:       const linear=[this.sum[o]/s,this.sum[o+1]/s,this.sum[o+2]/s];
8463:       const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
8464:       rgba[p]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
8465:       rgba[p+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
8466:       rgba[p+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
8467:       rgba[p+3]=(settings.transparent&&!primaryMask[i])?0:255;
8468:     }
8469:     return rgba;
8470:   }
8471:   averageSamples(){
8472:     let total=0;for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++)total+=this.samples[i];
8473:     return total/Math.max(1,this.samples.length);
8474:   }
8475:   activeIndices(settings){
8476:     const out=new Uint32Array(this.active||this.samples.length);
8477:     let n=0;
8478:     for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
8479:       if(this.shouldSample(i,settings))out[n++]=i;
8480:       else this.markConverged(i);
8481:     }

8419: class ProgressiveAccumulator{
8420:   constructor(width,height){
8421:     this.width=width;this.height=height;
8422:     const n=width*height;
8423:     this.sum=new Float64Array(n*3);
8424:     this.mean=new Float64Array(n);
8425:     this.m2=new Float64Array(n);
8426:     this.samples=new Uint32Array(n);
8427:     this.converged=new Uint8Array(n);
8428:     this.active=n;
8429:   }
8430:   add(index,color){
8431:     const s=++this.samples[index];
8432:     const o=index*3;
8433:     this.sum[o]+=color[0];this.sum[o+1]+=color[1];this.sum[o+2]+=color[2];
8434:     const lum=.2126*color[0]+.7152*color[1]+.0722*color[2];
8435:     const delta=lum-this.mean[index];
8436:     this.mean[index]+=delta/s;
8437:     this.m2[index]+=delta*(lum-this.mean[index]);
8438:   }
8439:   variance(index){
8440:     const s=this.samples[index];
8441:     return s>1?Math.max(0,this.m2[index]/(s-1)):Infinity;
8442:   }
8443:   noise(index){
8444:     const mean=Math.max(1e-6,Math.abs(this.mean[index]));
8445:     const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[index]);
8446:     return Math.sqrt(this.variance(index)/s)/mean;
8447:   }
8448:   shouldSample(index,settings){
8449:     if(!settings.adaptive)return this.samples[index]<settings.maxSamples;
8450:     if(this.samples[index]<settings.minSamples)return true;
8451:     if(this.samples[index]>=settings.maxSamples)return false;
8452:     return this.noise(index)>settings.noiseThreshold;
8453:   }
8454:   markConverged(index){
8455:     if(this.converged[index])return;
8456:     this.converged[index]=1;this.active=Math.max(0,this.active-1);
8457:   }
8458:   resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask){
8459:     const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(this.width*this.height*4);
8460:     for(let i=0;i<this.width*this.height;i++){
8461:       const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[i]),o=i*3,p=i*4;
8462:       const linear=[this.sum[o]/s,this.sum[o+1]/s,this.sum[o+2]/s];
8463:       const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
8464:       rgba[p]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
8465:       rgba[p+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
8466:       rgba[p+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
8467:       rgba[p+3]=(settings.transparent&&!primaryMask[i])?0:255;
8468:     }
8469:     return rgba;
8470:   }
8471:   averageSamples(){
8472:     let total=0;for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++)total+=this.samples[i];
8473:     return total/Math.max(1,this.samples.length);
8474:   }
8475:   activeIndices(settings){
8476:     const out=new Uint32Array(this.active||this.samples.length);
8477:     let n=0;
8478:     for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
8479:       if(this.shouldSample(i,settings))out[n++]=i;
8480:       else this.markConverged(i);
8481:     }
8482:     return out.subarray(0,n);
8483:   }
8484:   compactActive(settings){
8485:     return this.activeIndices(settings);
8486:   }
8487: 
8488:   averageNoise(){
8489:     let sum=0,count=0;
8490:     for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
8491:       if(this.samples[i]>1&&Number.isFinite(this.noise(i))){sum+=this.noise(i);count++;}

8419: class ProgressiveAccumulator{
8420:   constructor(width,height){
8421:     this.width=width;this.height=height;
8422:     const n=width*height;
8423:     this.sum=new Float64Array(n*3);
8424:     this.mean=new Float64Array(n);
8425:     this.m2=new Float64Array(n);
8426:     this.samples=new Uint32Array(n);
8427:     this.converged=new Uint8Array(n);
8428:     this.active=n;
8429:   }
8430:   add(index,color){
8431:     const s=++this.samples[index];
8432:     const o=index*3;
8433:     this.sum[o]+=color[0];this.sum[o+1]+=color[1];this.sum[o+2]+=color[2];
8434:     const lum=.2126*color[0]+.7152*color[1]+.0722*color[2];
8435:     const delta=lum-this.mean[index];
8436:     this.mean[index]+=delta/s;
8437:     this.m2[index]+=delta*(lum-this.mean[index]);
8438:   }
8439:   variance(index){
8440:     const s=this.samples[index];
8441:     return s>1?Math.max(0,this.m2[index]/(s-1)):Infinity;
8442:   }
8443:   noise(index){
8444:     const mean=Math.max(1e-6,Math.abs(this.mean[index]));
8445:     const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[index]);
8446:     return Math.sqrt(this.variance(index)/s)/mean;
8447:   }
8448:   shouldSample(index,settings){
8449:     if(!settings.adaptive)return this.samples[index]<settings.maxSamples;
8450:     if(this.samples[index]<settings.minSamples)return true;
8451:     if(this.samples[index]>=settings.maxSamples)return false;
8452:     return this.noise(index)>settings.noiseThreshold;
8453:   }
8454:   markConverged(index){
8455:     if(this.converged[index])return;
8456:     this.converged[index]=1;this.active=Math.max(0,this.active-1);
8457:   }
8458:   resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask){
8459:     const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(this.width*this.height*4);
8460:     for(let i=0;i<this.width*this.height;i++){
8461:       const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[i]),o=i*3,p=i*4;
8462:       const linear=[this.sum[o]/s,this.sum[o+1]/s,this.sum[o+2]/s];
8463:       const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
8464:       rgba[p]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
8465:       rgba[p+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
8466:       rgba[p+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
8467:       rgba[p+3]=(settings.transparent&&!primaryMask[i])?0:255;
8468:     }
8469:     return rgba;
8470:   }
8471:   averageSamples(){
8472:     let total=0;for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++)total+=this.samples[i];
8473:     return total/Math.max(1,this.samples.length);
8474:   }
8475:   activeIndices(settings){
8476:     const out=new Uint32Array(this.active||this.samples.length);
8477:     let n=0;
8478:     for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
8479:       if(this.shouldSample(i,settings))out[n++]=i;
8480:       else this.markConverged(i);
8481:     }
8482:     return out.subarray(0,n);
8483:   }
8484:   compactActive(settings){
8485:     return this.activeIndices(settings);

23256:   const AdaptiveRenderLearningController=Object.freeze({
23257:     version:VERSION,
23258:     score(sample={}){
23259:       const noise=clamp(safe(sample.noise,1),0,1);
23260:       const uncertainty=clamp(safe(sample.uncertainty,1),0,1);
23261:       const contribution=clamp(safe(sample.expectedContribution,1),0,1);
23262:       const success=clamp(safe(sample.historicalSuccess,1),0,1);
23263:       const visual=clamp(safe(sample.visualImportance,1),0,1);
23264:       const material=clamp(safe(sample.materialNeed,1),0,1);
23265:       const passNeed=clamp(safe(sample.passNeed,1),0,1);
23266:       const cost=Math.max(.001,safe(sample.estimatedCost,1));
23267:       return clamp((noise*uncertainty*contribution*success*visual*material*passNeed)/cost,0,1);
23268:     },
23269:     passDecay(pass=1,maxPasses=64){
23270:       const p=Math.max(1,safe(pass,1)),m=Math.max(1,safe(maxPasses,64));
23271:       const t=clamp((p-1)/Math.max(1,m-1),0,1);
23272:       return clamp(1-(.9*Math.pow(t,.72)),.10,1);
23273:     },
23274:     nextStrategy(feedback={}){
23275:       const pass=Math.max(1,safe(feedback.pass,1));
23276:       const maxPasses=Math.max(pass,safe(feedback.maxPasses,64));
23277:       const noise=clamp(safe(feedback.noise,1),0,1);
23278:       const stable=clamp(safe(feedback.stablePixelRatio,0),0,1);
23279:       const useful=clamp(safe(feedback.usefulRayRatio,1),0.05,1);
23280:       const efficiency=Math.max(0,safe(feedback.efficiency,1));
23281:       const decay=this.passDecay(pass,maxPasses);
23282:       const unresolved=clamp(1-stable,0.02,1);
23283:       const efficiencyScale=efficiency>0?clamp(.65+Math.min(.35,efficiency),.65,1):.65;
23284:       const rayBudget=clamp(decay*(.35+.65*noise)*unresolved*(.65+.35*useful)*efficiencyScale,.05,1);
23285:       return Object.freeze({
23286:         pass, maxPasses, rayBudget,
23287:         activePixelRatio:unresolved,
23288:         tilePriority:'noise-descending',
23289:         lightSampling:noise<.18?'importance-reservoir':'importance',
23290:         shadowBudget:clamp(rayBudget*(noise>.25?1:.65),.05,1),
23291:         bounceBudget:clamp(rayBudget*(useful<.35?.65:1),.05,1),
23292:         reflectionBudget:clamp(rayBudget*(safe(feedback.reflectionNoise,noise)>.15?1:.55),.05,1),
23293:         giBudget:clamp(rayBudget*(safe(feedback.giNoise,noise)>.15?1:.55),.05,1),
23294:         reusePrimaryHits:true,
23295:         reuseGBuffer:true,
23296:         reuseLightVisibility:true,
23297:         convergenceSkipThreshold:clamp(.0025+(pass/maxPasses)*.0125,.0025,.015),
23298:         denoiseAssist:pass>=Math.max(4,Math.floor(maxPasses*.25)),
23299:         learningMode:'quality-per-millisecond'
23300:       });
23301:     },
23302:     capture(job=null){
23303:       const j=job||window.RenderFramework?.jobs?.activeJob||window.RenderFramework?.jobs?.lastJob||null;
23304:       const p=j?.progress||{};
23305:       const perf=j?.performance||j?.metrics||{};
23306:       const pass=safe(p.currentSamples||p.currentPass||perf.samples,1);
23307:       const maxPasses=safe(p.maximumSamples||j?.settings?.samples,64);
23308:       return Object.freeze({
23309:         pass,maxPasses,
23310:         noise:clamp(safe(p.noise,1),0,1),
23311:         stablePixelRatio:clamp(safe(j?.adaptiveLearning?.stablePixelRatio,0),0,1),
23312:         usefulRayRatio:clamp(safe(j?.adaptiveLearning?.usefulRayRatio,1),0,1),
23313:         efficiency:safe(j?.adaptiveLearning?.efficiency,1),
